Extract earthquake features\n    earthquakes = []\n    for feature in data['features']:\n        props = feature['properties']\n        coords = feature['geometry']['coordinates']\n\n        earthquakes.append({\n            'id': feature['id'],\n            'place': props['place'],\n            'time': props['time'],\n            'magnitude': props['mag'],\n            'longitude': coords[0],\n            'latitude': coords[1],\n            'geometry': Point(coords[0], coords[1])\n        })\n\n    # Create GeoDataFrame with WGS84 (EPSG:4326)\n    gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(earthquakes, crs='EPSG:4326')\n    return gdf\n\ndef load_plate_boundaries(file_path):\n    \"\"\"Load plate boundary data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(file_path, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    boundaries = []\n    for feature in data['features']:\n        props = feature['properties']\n        geom = shape(feature['geometry'])\n\n        boundaries.append({\n            'name': props.get('Name', ''),\n            'plate_a': props.get('PlateA', ''),\n            'plate_b': props.get('PlateB', ''),\n            'geometry': geom\n        })\n\n    # Create GeoDataFrame with WGS84 (EPSG:4326)\n    gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(boundaries, crs='EPSG:4326')\n    return gdf\n\ndef load_plate_polygons(file_path):\n    \"\"\"Load plate polygon data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(file_path, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    plates = []\n    for feature in data['features']:\n        props = feature['properties']\n        geom = shape(feature['geometry'])\n\n        plates.append({\n            'code': props.get('Code', ''),\n            'name': props.get('PlateName', ''),\n            'geometry': geom\n        })\n\n    # Create GeoDataFrame with WGS84 (EPSG:4326)\n    gdf = gpd.GeoDataFrame(plates, crs='EPSG:4326')\n    return gdf\n\ndef find_pacific_boundaries(boundaries_gdf):\n    \"\"\"Find all boundaries that involve the Pacific plate (PA).\"\"\"\n    pacific_boundaries = boundaries_gdf[\n        (boundaries_gdf['plate_a'] == 'PA') |\n        (boundaries_gdf['plate_b'] == 'PA')\n    ].copy()\n\n    return pacific_boundaries\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    earthquakes = load_earthquake_data('/root/earthquakes_2024.json')\n    boundaries = load_plate_boundaries('/root/PB2002_boundaries.json')\n    plates = load_plate_polygons('/root/PB2002_plates.json')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(earthquakes)} earthquakes\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(boundaries)} plate boundaries\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(plates)} tectonic plates\")\n\n    # Find Pacific plate\n    pacific_plate = plates[plates['code'] == 'PA'].copy()\n    print(f\"\\nFound Pacific plate: {pacific_plate['name'].values[0]}\")\n\n    # Find Pacific plate boundaries\n    pacific_boundaries = find_pacific_boundaries(boundaries)\n    print(f\"Found {len(pacific_boundaries)} Pacific plate boundaries\")\n\n    # Combine all Pacific boundaries into a single geometry\n    pacific_boundary_geom = pacific_boundaries.unary_union\n\n    # Filter earthquakes that are within the Pacific plate\n    print(\"\\nFiltering earthquakes within Pacific plate...\")\n    earthquakes_in_pacific = gpd.sjoin(\n        earthquakes,\n        pacific_plate[['geometry']],\n        how='inner',\n        predicate='within'\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(earthquakes_in_pacific)} earthquakes within Pacific plate\")\n\n    if len(earthquakes_in_pacific) == 0:\n        print(\"No earthquakes found within Pacific plate!\")\n        return\n\n    # Use an appropriate projection for distance calculations\n    # For Pacific-wide analysis, we'll use a custom equidistant conic projection\n    # centered on the Pacific plate, or use EPSG:3832 (Pacific-centered equal distance)\n    # For accuracy across the Pacific, we'll use a Pacific-centered projection\n\n    # Project to Pacific-centered equal distance projection (EPSG:3832)\n    # This is WGS 84 / PDC Mercator - good for Pacific region\n    # Actually, let's use a custom equal-area projection centered on Pacific\n\n    # For the Pacific Ocean, we'll use EPSG:3832 (WGS 84 / PDC Mercator)\n    # Or we can use a custom Azimuthal Equidistant projection centered on the Pacific\n\n    # Let's use a Pacific-centered Azimuthal Equidistant projection\n    # Center approximately at the middle of the Pacific Ocean\n    pacific_center_lon = -170\n    pacific_center_lat = -10\n\n    # Define custom projection string\n    proj_string = f'+proj=aeqd +lat_0={pacific_center_lat} +lon_0={pacific_center_lon} +x_0=0 +y_0=0 +datum=WGS84 +units=m +no_defs'\n\n    print(f\"\\nProjecting to Pacific-centered Azimuthal Equidistant projection...\")\n    print(f\"Center: ({pacific_center_lat}°N, {pacific_center_lon}°E)\")\n\n    # Project all data to the custom projection\n    earthquakes_proj = earthquakes_in_pacific.to_crs(proj_string)\n    pacific_boundaries_proj = pacific_boundaries.to_crs(proj_string)\n\n    # Combine boundaries into single geometry in projected CRS\n    pacific_boundary_proj = pacific_boundaries_proj.unary_union\n\n    print(\"\\nCalculating distances to Pacific plate boundary...\")\n\n    # Calculate distance from each earthquake to the Pacific plate boundary\n    distances = []\n    for idx, eq in earthquakes_proj.iterrows():\n        dist_meters = eq.geometry.distance(pacific_boundary_proj)\n        dist_km = dist_meters / 1000.0\n        distances.append(dist_km)\n\n    earthquakes_proj['distance_km'] = distances\n\n    # Find the earthquake with maximum distance\n    max_dist_idx = earthquakes_proj['distance_km'].idxmax()\n    furthest_eq = earthquakes_proj.loc[max_dist_idx]\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(\"EARTHQUAKE FURTHEST FROM PACIFIC PLATE BOUNDARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n    print(f\"ID: {furthest_eq['id']}\")\n    print(f\"Place: {furthest_eq['place']}\")\n    print(f\"Magnitude: {furthest_eq['magnitude']}\")\n    print(f\"Latitude: {furthest_eq['latitude']:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Longitude: {furthest_eq['longitude']:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Distance to boundary: {furthest_eq['distance_km']:.2f} km\")\n\n    # Convert time from milliseconds to ISO 8601 format\n    time_ms = furthest_eq['time']\n    time_dt = datetime.utcfromtimestamp(time_ms / 1000.0)\n    time_iso = time_dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')\n    print(f\"Time: {time_iso}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n\n    # Prepare output\n    output = {\n        'id': furthest_eq['id'],\n        'place': furthest_eq['place'],\n        'time': time_iso,\n        'magnitude': float(furthest_eq['magnitude']),\n        'latitude': round(float(furthest_eq['latitude']), 6),\n        'longitude': round(float(furthest_eq['longitude']), 6),\n        'distance_km': round(float(furthest_eq['distance_km']), 2)\n    }\n\n    # Write to JSON file\n    with open('/root/answer.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to /root/answer.json\")\n\n    # Show top 5 for verification\n    print(\"\\nTop 5 earthquakes furthest from Pacific boundary:\")\n    top5 = earthquakes_proj.nlargest(5, 'distance_km')[\n        ['id', 'place', 'magnitude', 'distance_km']\n    ]\n    for idx, row in top5.iterrows():\n        print(f\"  {row['id']}: {row['place'][:50]:50s} - {row['distance_km']:.2f} km\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":263,"cache_read_input_tokens":57297,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":263,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3dab51fd-06c5-4a9a-9c29-e1fdc5f0466d","uuid":"44154cc4-454c-4e8b-a495-25ff09704348"}
